Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a time_series algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 745. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 950/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 745 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the time_series structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 950. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
